```
63	export function applyProjStatus(projId: number | undefined, enemy: StatusEnemy, ctx?: ProjStatusCtx): void {
64	  if (projId === undefined || projId < 0) return;
65	  const entries = TABLE[String(projId)];
66	  if (!entries) return;
67	  let pendingMut: { id: number; roll: number } | null = null;

71	    if (en.gate === 'setHuntressT2' && !ctx?.setHuntressT2) continue;
72	    let hit: boolean;
73	    if (en.mut !== undefined) {
74	      // 互斥组:同组共用一次掷骰,按累积 chance 区间判定本条是否被选中
75	      if (!pendingMut || pendingMut.id !== en.mut) pendingMut = { id: en.mut, roll: Math.random() };
76	      let lo = 0;
77	      hit = false;
78	      for (const g of entries) {
79	        if (g.mut !== en.mut) continue;
80	        const hi = lo + g.chance;
81	        if (g === en) { hit = pendingMut.roll >= lo && pendingMut.roll < hi; break; }
82	        lo = hi;
83	      }
84	    } else {
85	      hit = Math.random() < en.chance;
86	    }
87	    if (!hit) continue;
88	    // NPC buffImmune 门（NPC.cs:90968 AddBuff 免疫即 return + NPCID.Sets

91	    if (vId !== undefined && vId > 0 && npcImmuneTo(vId, en.buff)) continue;
92	    const field = SUPPORTED[en.buff];
93	    if (!field) continue; // 未实装 buff,登记见上
94	    const ticks = en.durMin + Math.floor(Math.random() * (en.durMax - en.durMin + 1));
95	    const sec = ticks / 60;
96	    const cur = enemy[field] ?? 0;
97	    if (sec > cur) enemy[field] = sec;
98	  }
99	}
```

```
	public int checkArmorPenetration(int armorPenetration, float armorPenetrationPercent)
	{
		if (ichor)
		{
			armorPenetration += 15;
		}
		if (brokenArmor)
		{
			armorPenetration += 20;
		}
		if (betsysCurse)
		{
			armorPenetration += 40;
		}
		armorPenetration += (int)((float)defense * armorPenetrationPercent);
		if (armorPenetration <= 0 || defense <= 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		if (armorPenetration > defense)
		{
			return defense / 2;
		}
		return armorPenetration / 2;
	}

	public double StrikeNPCNoInteraction(int Damage, float knockBack, int hitDirection, bool crit = false, bool noEffect = false, bool fromNet = false)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 0)
		{
			ignorePlayerInteractions++;
		}
		return StrikeNPC(Damage, knockBack, hitDirection, crit, noEffect, fromNet, 255);
```

```
namespace Terraria.Audio;

public class VampireSizzleTracker
{
	private int _playerIndex;

	public VampireSizzleTracker(int whoAmI)
	{
		_playerIndex = whoAmI;
	}

	public bool IsActiveAndInGame()
	{
		if (Main.gameMenu || !Main.vampireSeed)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return Main.player[_playerIndex].sunScorchCounter > 0;
	}
}
```

```
  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, kby: number, game: GameHooks, armorPen = 0, crit = false, pierce = 0, fromPlayer = false, penPercent = 0): boolean {
    // pierce 语义（Projectile.cs:11903-11907/13152-13155 Damage_PVE）：
    //   0   = 近战/荆棘等非弹幕 → 命中设免疫 10t（原版 immune[owner] 默认档）；
    //   1   = 单发弹（maxPenetrate==1，flag3）→ **无视免疫且命中后不设帧**
    //         ——同 tick 多发（霰弹/多随从）全部生效,不再互挡（旧全局 iframes=8
    //         致 8 发四管霰弹只吃 1 发,DPS≈1/8,2026-08-13 修）；
    //   >1  = 穿透弹 → 设 10t;同弹重复命中由弹侧 hitSet 拦（原版 per-owner 槽单人
    //         近似;弹型覆写 1-8 表 :13032 未接,登记）
    // Critter 兼容：critters 桶的调用方按 Critter.hurt(game) 单参调用；
    // 原版小动物是 Enemy 进该桶，这里把 Game 对象重映射回 game 参、伤害取固定 5
    if (typeof dmg === 'object' && dmg !== null) {
      game = dmg as unknown as GameHooks;
      dmg = 5; kbx = 0; kby = 0;
    }
    const rawDmg = dmg; // 傀儡上报用原始值（下方 def 凘认算只做一次，双端一致）
    // 死亡单次门(用户报"多弹头武器打出双份僵尸碎块"):pierce=1 豁免免疫帧后,
    // 同 tick 多弹头(霰弹/食人鱼咬+随从)会全部进 hurt——首发致死后,后续弹头
    // 对"已死未出列"的敌怪二次触发死亡管线(碎块/掉落/音效翻倍)。原版由
    // Damage_PVE 的 npc.active 前置门(Projectile.cs:11869)天然防重;此处等价。
    if (this.dead) return false;
    if (pierce !== 1 && this.iframes > 0) return false; // 单发弹豁免免疫检查（flag3）
    // 旗 buff 加伤（Main.GetBannerBuffEffect → StrikeNPC 乘区,ItemID.cs:11-44 普通族:
    // 经典 ×1.5/专家 ×2;DD2 0.2 族不区分,登记）——统一入口=StrikeNPC 全通道语义
    if (this.vanillaId != null && (game as { bannerNpcActive?: (n: number) => boolean }).bannerNpcActive?.(this.vanillaId)) {
      const g = game as { world?: { flags?: Record<string, boolean> } };
      dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * (g.world?.flags?.hardMode ? 2 : 1.5)));
    }
    if (this.dontTakeDamage) return false; // 原版 NPC.dontTakeDamage（StrikeNPC 早退）
    this.justHitT = 2; // 原版 justHit 单帧旗标（AI_023 飞行武器/陆龟受击回态消费）
    // 训练假人（NPC 488，cs:83498 localAI 赋值）：immortal 永不死，
    // 受击只记摇晃强度+方向（渲染帧端消费），掉血由 dummyAI 回满
    if (this.vanillaId === 488) {
      this.dummyShake = Math.max(20, Math.min(120, dmg));
      this.dummyHitDir = Math.sign(kbx) || this.dummyHitDir;
      this.iframes = 8;
      return true;
    }
    // 猪鲨爆裂泡泡（NPC 371，StrikeNPC cs:82102-82107）：伤害清零(num=0)不扣血，
    // 直接转入爆炸态 ai[0]=1/ai[1]=4 + dontTakeDamage——"被击即爆"而非"被打死"；
    // aiStyle 70 (:35180-35194) 据此扩 100×100 并 EncourageDespawn(3) 消散
    if (this.vanillaId === 371) {
      this.ai0 = 1; this.ai1 = 4;
      this.iframes = 8; // dontTakeDamage（爆炸 4t 内不可再受击）
      return true;
    }
    // 原版 Main.CalculateDamageNPCsTake：dmg - defense*0.5，下限 1
    const def = this.def.defense ?? 0;
    // ichor（灵液药剂）：NPC.checkArmorPenetration（NPC.cs:81913-81938）——
    // armorPenetration += 15，结算 (armorPenetration/2) 整除 = +7 直伤；
    // 超防钳制（def>2×dmg 时按 dmg 上限，防穿透把伤害推到全额以上）
    // ⑥ armorPen 改减防语义（NPC.cs:81913-81938 checkArmorPenetration=min(pen,def)：
    // 有效防 = def - min(armorPen,def)，伤 = dmg - 有效防/2——此前调用方把 pen 加在
    // 伤害上约 2 倍过强；ichor 灵液仍走 +7 直伤通道（:81920 既有特例）
    const pen = this.ichorT > 0 ? 7 : 0;
    const effDef0 = def > dmg * 2 ? dmg * 2 : def;
    // 负防御(EoC P2 专家 -15/-30、诸 Boss 狂暴态)按原版直算式应为【增伤】;
    // 此前 Math.max(0,…) 把负值钳成 0 吞掉加伤(2026-08-13 对齐)。armorPen 只削
    // 非负防御(min 内侧钳 0),不与负防御交互
    // buff 侧无视防(NPC.cs:81919-81924):BrokenArmor +20 / BetsysCurse +40 armorPenetration
    const buffPen = (this.brokenArmorT > 0 ? 20 : 0) + (this.betsysCurseT > 0 ? 40 : 0);
    // armorPenetrationPercent（:12443 闪电光环 688/690=1:按比例无视防御——在定点穿甲前扣）
    const pctDef = Math.max(0, effDef0) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, penPercent));
    const effDef = effDef0 - pctDef - Math.min(armorPen + buffPen, Math.max(0, effDef0) - pctDef);
    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - effDef * 0.5 + pen));
    // takenDamageMultiplier（NPC.StrikeNPC cs:81997-82000）：防御结算后 >1 时 ×N
    // （419 旋刃怪冲刺中 ×3，cs:57172）
    if (this.takenDamageMultiplier > 1) dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * this.takenDamageMultiplier));
    // realLife 共享生命（StrikeNPC :82132-82137）：伤害扣主段,本段 hp/maxHp 镜像
    // ——毁灭者族打任意节都扣头 80000 总血,节不可单独打死
    if (this.realLifeHost && !this.realLifeHost.dead) {
      this.realLifeHost.hp -= dmg;
      this.hp = this.realLifeHost.hp;
      this.maxHp = this.realLifeHost.maxHp;
    } else {
      this.hp -= dmg;
    }
    // DPS 计累计(Projectile.cs:12817-12818/Player.cs:20165-20168 两调用点均
    // accDreamCatcher 门禁+fromPlayer 标记;全仓伤害漏斗单点收口)
    if (fromPlayer) {
      const pl = game.player as Player | null;
      pl?.addDPS(dmg);
      this.playerInteracted = true;  // AnyInteractions（playerInteraction 位）——雕像门 :79651
    }
    // 毁灭者身段受击出探针（HitEffect :90265-90281：135 身段存活受击时,ai[2]==0
    // （本段未出过探针）掷 1/25（Mechdusa 合体态 1/50）→ 置 ai[2]=1 并于段底中心
    // 生成探针 139——每身段至多一只）
    if (this.vanillaId === 135 && this.hp > 0 && this.ai2 === 0) {
      const max = mechQueenUp() ? 50 : 25;
      if (Math.floor(Math.random() * max) === 0) {
        this.ai2 = 1;
        const probe = Enemy.fromVanilla(139, this.cx, this.y + this.h);
        if (probe) addEnemy(game, probe);
      }
    }
    this.iframes = pierce === 1 ? 0 : 10; // 通用档 10t（:13152 默认;单发弹不设帧 flag3;旧恒 8）
    this.hpBarT = 240; // 受击后血条持续显示 4 秒
    // 击退管线（N:82144-82236 1:1）：五段软封顶 8/10/12/14/16（超出段 ×0.9/0.8/
    // 0.7/0.6/钳 16）+ 地狱火(onFire2,onFireT 代)×1.1 + 暴击×1.4 + 小丑 185×1.5。
    // ★调用侧遗留的 0.65 系数待全量清扫后此管线即完整 num3 语义（登记）
    let imp = Math.hypot(kbx, kby);
    if (imp > 0) {
      if (this.onFireT > 0) imp *= 1.1;
      if (imp > 8) imp = 8 + (imp - 8) * 0.9;
      if (imp > 10) imp = 10 + (imp - 10) * 0.8;
      if (imp > 12) imp = 12 + (imp - 12) * 0.7;
      if (imp > 14) imp = 14 + (imp - 14) * 0.6;
      if (imp > 16) imp = 16;
      if (crit) imp *= 1.4;
      if (this.vanillaId === 185) imp *= 1.5;
      const f = imp / Math.hypot(kbx, kby);
      kbx *= f; kby *= f;
    }
    // 应用（N:82142-82239）：门 knockBackResist>0（0=免疫 Boss 整块跳过）；
    // num3=kb×resist 一次乘 + 五段封顶 + crit×1.4。kby===0 = 武器路径 →
    // 重路（num8=(int)伤×10(专家15)≤lifeMax 时）：X 收敛到 ±num3（:82204-82216
    // 同向不足才推、反向 ×2 步、钳目标值）+ Y=-num3×0.75×resist（:82231 二次乘）；
    // TODO 轻路（num8>lifeMax 大伤害打小怪）：减法收敛 + 185 特例 + Y 加法钳。
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-14T09:15:15.629Z · glm-x-preview-260804

````
Now I understand the current repo implementation in Enemy.hurt:

```ts
const pen = this.ichorT > 0 ? 7 : 0;
const effDef0 = def > dmg * 2 ? dmg * 2 : def;
const buffPen = (this.brokenArmorT > 0 ? 20 : 0) + (this.betsysCurseT > 0 ? 40 : 0);
const pctDef = Math.max(0, effDef0) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, penPercent));
const effDef = effDef0 - pctDef - Math.min(armorPen + buffPen, Math.max(0, effDef0) - pctDef);
dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - effDef * 0.5 + pen));
```

Vanilla (NPC.cs:81913-81944 + Projectile.cs:12068,12808):
```cs
int num3 = Main.player[owner].GetArmorPenetration(melee) + armorPenetration;  // :12068
...
num12 += targetNPC.checkArmorPenetration(num3, armorPenetrationPercent);  // :12808

checkArmorPenetration(pen, pct):
  if (ichor) pen += 15;
  if (brokenArmor) pen += 20;
  if (betsysCurse) pen += 40;
  pen += (int)(defense * pct);
  if (pen <= 0 || defense <= 0) return 0;
  if (pen > defense) return defense / 2;
  return pen / 2;
```

So vanilla: damage = base dmg (then via StrikeNPC: dmg = num12 - defense/2 etc.) where num12 has been increased by min-ish(pen/2, defense/2).

Wait — how does StrikeNPC use num12? In vanilla StrikeNPC: `num = Main.CalculateDamageNPCsTake(num, defense, ...)`? Let me check. Actually StrikeNPC applies `damage = damage - defense/2` somewhere. In vanilla, the flow is:

1. num12 = damage * modifiers (crit etc.)
2. num12 += checkArmorPenetration(num3, pct) — adds a damage BONUS equal to pen/2 (capped at defense/2)
3. Then StrikeNPC(num12, ...) → inside, CalculateDamageNPCsTake: damage - defense*0.5.

Hmm wait, that means vanilla armor pen ADDS damage rather than reducing defense: net damage = (dmg + min(pen,def)/2) - def/2 = dmg - (def - min(pen,def))/2. Which is exactly equivalent to reducing effective defense by min(pen,def)! dmg - effDef/2 where effDef = def - min(pen, def).

So repo's model: `effDef = effDef0 - pctDef - min(armorPen + buffPen, max(0,effDef0) - pctDef)` and `dmg = dmg - effDef*0.5 + pen` where pen = ichor ? 7 : 0.

Equivalence check with vanilla:
- Vanilla total pen = num3 (player.GetArmorPenetration(melee) + proj.armorPenetration) + 15 if ichor + 20 brokenArmor + 40 betsys + defense*pct.
- The repo: armorPen (param) presumably = player armorPen + proj armorPen (set at call sites in Game.ts — I can't change those, but they already pass equipStats.armorPen + meleeArmorPen etc.). buffPen = brokenArmor + betsys — these get added into the same min() with armorPen. Vanilla also sums them into one pen before min with defense. So `min(armorPen + buffPen, def)` ≈ vanilla's `min(totalPen, def)` — equivalent, since min(sum, def) = min over total.
- pct: vanilla adds `(int)(defense * pct)` INTO pen before clamping (so percent-based pen is also capped by min(pen,def)/2 damage add, i.e. cap at defense/2). Repo subtracts pctDef separately BEFORE the min, and clamps the min against the remainder — `min(armorPen+buffPen, max(0,effDef0) - pctDef)`. This is equivalent to min(total, def) where total = pct*def + armorPen + buffPen, EXCEPT: vanilla pct applies to raw `defense` (not effDef0 = min(def, 2*dmg)), and vanilla's floor... Repo uses effDef0 (clamped def) for pct. Minor.
- Ichor: vanilla ichor adds +15 to pen → damage bonus = min(...,def)/2 increases by up to 7 (15/2=7.5→7 integer division). Repo does `+ pen` where pen=7 as a direct damage add AFTER effDef subtraction. Hmm: repo: dmg = dmg - effDef*0.5 + 7. Vanilla: dmg = dmg - (def - min(total,def))/2 where total includes ichor's 15.

Difference: in repo, ichor's +7 is a flat unconditional add, even if defense is 0 or already fully penetrated by armorPen. In vanilla, ichor's +15 participates in the capped min: if armorPen already >= def, ichor adds nothing extra (cap def/2). Also vanilla has the early return: if pen <= 0 || defense <= 0 → return 0 (no bonus). With defense <= 0 (negative defense), no pen bonus at all. Repo handles "armorPen only cuts non-negative defense" but the ichor +7 flat add bypasses that — with def=0, vanilla gives 0 bonus, repo gives +7. With negative defense... repo +7 also applies. That's the "混算" (mixed computation) problem: ichor is a fixed +7 direct damage regardless of cap, instead of participating in the shared capped pool.

So the correct 1:1 fix: make ichor contribute +15 to the shared pen pool, and remove the flat +7. That's all inside Enemy.hurt — which is FORBIDDEN.
````

```
  private thornBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
    };
    let bounced = false;
    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
      this.x -= this.vx;
      this.vx *= -0.9;
      bounced = true;
    }
    if (this.vy > 3 && !solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
      this.y -= this.vy;
      this.vy *= -0.9;
      bounced = true;
    }
    return bounced;
  }

  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。
```

```
  private spawnSidekick(game: GameHooks, owner: Player, itemId: number): void {

    const projs = (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: MinionProj[]; add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;

      false, 0, false, () => (owner.dead ? null : owner));
    m.noSlot = true;
    projs.add(m, 'projectiles');
  }

      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(arm.x, arm.y);
      for (const p of pts) ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      return;
    }
    ctx.save();
```

```
			if (insideUnbreakableWalls != flag && Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				UnbreakableWallScan.NetModule.BroadcastChange(this);

		}
```

```
584:  private rateLeft = new Map<string, number>();
585:  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
586:    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
587:    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
588:    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
589:    let heal = 0;

596:      while (left <= -chunk + 1e-9) { left += chunk; heal -= chunk; }
```

```
Very interesting! The repo ALREADY implements the vanilla Bleeding model at Player.ts:1206-1230:
- `lifeRegenTime` ramp counter with base +1/tick, honey +2, aegis crystal +0.2
- Bleeding/Obstructed clear lifeRegenTime (`:18998`/`:11424`)
- sinceHurt is a separate "距上次受击 tick" counter (used for heart animation), and damage sets sinceHurt=0 AND lifeRegenTime=0 (`:37784`).

So the Buffs.ts:386 comment "前者由 damage() 的 sinceHurt=0 近似" is STALE — the approximation has already been superseded by a proper lifeRegenTime model (Player.ts:233-236, 1206-1230). The remaining bits in that comment:
```

```
43	  if (!/^[\s\d()&|=<>!*]+$/.test(stripped)) return null;

45	}
46	/** 平衡花括号取块体;返回 { text, end } 或 null(end = 块尾 } 的下一位) */

50	  let d = 0;
51	  for (let i = braceAt; i < text.length; i++) {
52	    if (text[i] === '{') d++;

85	{
86	  // 顶层链:if 关键字必须位于方法体语句层(其前的花括号深度为 0)

93	      if (body[i] === '{') d0++;
94	      else if (body[i] === '}') d0--;
95	    }
96	    if (d0 !== 0) continue; // 嵌套 if 由 collectLinesForId 处理
97	    const c = readCond(body, m.index + m[0].length - 1);
98	    if (!c) break;
99	    const fn = tryCompile(c.cond);
100	    const blk = readBlock(body, c.condEnd);
101	    if (!blk) { chainRe.lastIndex = c.condEnd; continue; }
102	    const block = blk.text;
103	    if (fn) {
104	      for (let id = 0; id <= MAX_ID; id++) {
105	        let hit = false;
106	        try { hit = !!fn(id); } catch { hit = false; }
107	        if (hit && !seen.has(id)) {
108	          seen.add(id);
109	          blocks.push({ id, block });
110	        }
111	      }
112	    }
113	    chainRe.lastIndex = c.condEnd;
114	  }
115	}
116	if (blocks.length < 500) throw new Error(`链块过少: ${blocks.length}，疑似源码结构变化`);
117	
118	// ---- 深度 1 赋值（块内语句深度 0） ----
119	const depth1Lines = (block) => {

123	    const opens = (raw.match(/\{/g) || []).length;
124	    const closes = (raw.match(/\}/g) || []).length;
125	    if (d === 0 && opens === 0) out.push(raw.trim());
126	    d += opens - closes;
127	    if (d < 0) d = 0;
128	  }
129	  return out;
130	};

132	const NUM_FIELDS = ['aiStyle', 'width', 'height', 'penetrate', 'timeLeft', 'damage', 'light', 'scale', 'extraUpdates'];
133	const BOOL_FIELDS = ['friendly', 'melee', 'ranged', 'magic', 'minion', 'hostile', 'tileCollide', 'ignoreWater', 'pierce'];
134	
135	// ---- DefaultTo* 辅助方法展开（1456 惯例：DefaultToSpear()/DefaultToYoyo()/DefaultToFlail() 等
136	//      内部设 aiStyle 19/99/15 —— 长矛/悠悠球/连枷不在链内直写 aiStyle，必须展开才有值） ----
137	const helper = {};
138	for (const hm of src.matchAll(/public void (DefaultTo\w+)\(\)\s*\{/g)) {
139	  const hs = src.indexOf('{', hm.index);
140	  let d = 0, he = hs;

142	    if (src[i] === '{') d++;
143	    else if (src[i] === '}') { d--; if (d === 0) { he = i; break; } }
144	  }
145	  const rec = {};
146	  const hlines = depth1Lines(src.slice(hs + 1, he));

148	    const line = hlines.find((l) => new RegExp(`^${f} = -?[\\d.]+f?;`).test(l));
149	    if (line) rec[f] = parseFloat(line.match(/-?[\d.]+/)[0]);
150	  }
151	  for (const f of BOOL_FIELDS) {
152	    if (hlines.some((l) => l === `${f} = true;`)) rec[f] = true;
153	    else if (hlines.some((l) => l === `${f} = false;`)) rec[f] = false;
154	  }
155	  helper[hm[1]] = rec;
156	}
157	// 展开顺序内的行序列（DefaultTo* 调用与直写赋值按出现顺序合并，后设覆盖）
158	const data = {};

161	  const lines = collectLinesForId(block, id);
162	  const rec = {};
163	  const apply = (ls) => {
164	    for (const f of NUM_FIELDS) {
165	      const line = ls.find((l) => new RegExp(`^${f} = -?[\\d.]+f?;`).test(l));
166	      if (line) rec[f] = parseFloat(line.match(/-?[\d.]+/)[0]);
167	    }
168	    for (const f of BOOL_FIELDS) {
169	      if (ls.some((l) => l === `${f} = true;`)) rec[f] = true;
170	      else if (ls.some((l) => l === `${f} = false;`)) rec[f] = false;
171	    }
172	  };
173	  for (const raw of lines) {
174	    const call = raw.match(/^(DefaultTo\w+)\(\);$/);
175	    if (call && helper[call[1]]) {
176	      // 展开辅助方法赋值（与直写同字段语义）
177	      const h = helper[call[1]];
178	      for (const f of NUM_FIELDS) if (h[f] !== undefined) rec[f] = h[f];
179	      for (const f of BOOL_FIELDS) if (h[f] !== undefined) rec[f] = h[f];
180	    }
181	  }
182	  apply(lines);
183	  if (Object.keys(rec).length) data[id] = { ...(data[id] || {}), ...rec }; // 区间与单值重叠时合并（后设覆盖）
184	}
```

```
	public static double CalculateDamageNPCsTake(int Damage, int Defense)
	{
		double num = (double)Damage - (double)Defense * 0.5;
		if (num < 1.0)
		{
			num = 1.0;
		}
		return num;
	}
```

```
// 4) Ironskin(5)：防御 +8（1456 数值）
// 5) 到期自动移除
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { BuffState, BuffType, BUFF_DEFS, buffTypeByVanilla, wetFromBubbleContact } from '../src/stats/Buffs';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';

describe('Buff 框架（AddBuff 语义）', () => {
  it('apply 取 max：续期不缩短、短时长不覆盖长时长', () => {
    const b = new BuffState();
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 10);          // 短不覆盖长
    expect(b.active.get(BuffType.Honey)).toBe(30);
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);          // 等值保持
    expect(b.active.get(BuffType.Honey)).toBe(30);
    b.tick(10);                            // 剩 20
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);          // 浸入续期回满
    expect(b.active.get(BuffType.Honey)).toBe(30);
  });

  it('到期自动移除', () => {
    const b = new BuffState();
    b.apply(BuffType.Regen, 1);
    b.tick(0.9);
    expect(b.has(BuffType.Regen)).toBe(true);
    b.tick(0.2);
    expect(b.has(BuffType.Regen)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('蜂蜜 Buff（48）', () => {
  it('浸入语义：1 HP/s 持续回复，30 秒窗口', () => {
    const b = new BuffState();
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);
    let healed = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 600; k++) healed += b.tick(1 / 60);   // 10 秒
    expect(healed).toBe(10);
  });

  it('离蜜后倒计时消退（不再回复）', () => {
    const b = new BuffState();
    b.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);
    let healed = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 30.5 * 60; k++) healed += b.tick(1 / 60);
    expect(healed).toBe(30);          // 30 秒恰好回 30 HP
    expect(b.has(BuffType.Honey)).toBe(false);
    const h2 = b.tick(1);
    expect(h2).toBe(0);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { applyProjStatus } from '../src/entities/projStatus';
import tableJson from '../src/data/vanilla-projstatus.json';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

const TABLE = tableJson as unknown as Record<string, Array<Record<string, number | string | undefined>>>;

function makeHooks() {
  const store = new TileStore(60, 60);
  const player = new Player(30 * 16, 30 * 16, new Inventory());
  const hooks: GameHooks = {
    world: { store } as never,
    player: player as never,
    enemies: () => [],
    critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null,
    damagePlayer: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {},
    cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {},
    spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {},
    notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {},
    playSfxFiles: () => {},
    showPickupLabel: () => {},
  };
  return hooks;
}

/** 新敌 + 1 秒步进,返回掉血量(字段直写,不经 AddBuff 免疫表) */
function dotLoss(field: string, hp = 1000): number {
  const e = new Enemy('zombie', 10 * 16, 10 * 16);
  e.iframes = 0; e.hp = hp; e.hpBarT = 0;
  (e as unknown as Record<string, number>)[field] = 1;
  e.fixedUpdate(1, makeHooks() as never);
  return hp - e.hp;
}
```

````
- Add exported 1:1 `checkArmorPenetration()` pure port? Hmm. Let me think about whether to include it. "把 armorPen 建成独立字段参与 NPC 有效防御计算" — the actual NPC-side calc is Enemy.hurt which I can't touch. A pure helper awaiting wiring + tests = concrete deliverable for next session. I'll add it to projStatus.ts (it's the debuff/settlement-adjacent module, already documents checkArmorPenetration). Keep it small:

```ts
/** NPC.checkArmorPenetration 1:1(NPC.cs:81913-81938)——StrikeNPC 伤害加成链:
 *  bonus = min(pool, def)/2 加在伤害上;pool = armorPen(玩家+弹幕) + ichor15 +
 *  brokenArmor20 + betsyCurse40 + (int)(def×pct);def≤0 或 pool≤0 → 0。
 *  (Enemy.hurt 待接线——热文件禁改,2026-08-14) */
export function npcCheckArmorPenetration(defense: number, armorPen: number, penPercent = 0, flags: {ichor?: boolean; brokenArmor?: boolean; betsysCurse?: boolean} = {}): number {
  let pen = armorPen;
  if (flags.ichor) pen += 15;
  if (flags.brokenArmor) pen += 20;
  if (flags.betsysCurse) pen += 40;
  pen += Math.trunc(defense * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, penPercent)));
  if (pen <= 0 || defense <= 0) return 0;
  if (pen > defense) return Math.trunc(defense / 2);
  return Math.trunc(pen / 2);
}
```
````

```json
 "new_string": "export function applyMeleeImbue(\n  buffs: { has: (b: never) => boolean },\n  enemy: StatusEnemy,\n  partyFx?: () => void,\n): void {\n  const has = (bt: BuffType): boolean => buffs.has(bt as unknown as never);\n  const max = (field: string, v: number): void => {\n    if (v > (enemy[field] ?? 0)) enemy[field] = v;\n  };\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueVenom)) max('venomT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueCursed)) max('cursedT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueFire)) max('onFireT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueGold)) max('midasT', 2);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueIchor)) max('ichorT', 10 + Math.random() * 10);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueNano)) max('confusedT', 1 + Math.random() * 3);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbuePoison)) max('poisonT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);\n  if (has(BuffType.ImbueParty)) partyFx?.();\n}\n\n/** NPC.checkArmorPenetration 1:1 纯函数（NPC.cs:81913-81938）。\n *  原版语义：返回值是【加到伤害上的加成】（StrikeNPC :12808 num12 += 本值，\n *  之后 CalculateDamageNPCsTake 才减 def/2——Main.cs:65626-65635），等价于\n *  有效防 = def − min(pool, def)。pool 单池累加（无\"每层独立\"档）：\n *    armorPenetration（StrikeNPC :12068 = 玩家 GetArmorPenetration(melee) +\n *    弹幕自带 armorPenetration——数据层 vanillaProjectiles.armorPenetration）\n *    + ichor 15（:81916）+ brokenArmor 20（:81919）+ betsysCurse 40（:81922）\n *    + (int)(defense × armorPenetrationPercent)（:81927，闪电光环 688/690 传 1）。\n *  早退：pool ≤ 0 或 defense ≤ 0 → 0（负防/零防不吃穿甲加成）；\n *  超防钳制：pool > defense → defense/2（加成永不把伤害推过全额）。\n *  ★待接线：Enemy.hurt 现为\"armorPen 减防 + ichor 平推 +7 直伤\"近似（池外混算），\n *  2026-08-14 Enemy.ts 热文件禁改——冷却后替换 hurt 内 :6551-6557 段为\n *  dmg + npcCheckArmorPenetration(def, armorPen, penPercent, flags) 结算链 */\nexport function npcCheckArmorPenetration(\n  defense: number,\n  armorPenetration: number,\n  armorPenetrationPercent = 0,\n  flags: { ichor?: boolean; brokenArmor?: boolean; betsysCurse?: boolean } = {},\n): number {\n  let pen = armorPenetration | 0;\n  if (flags.ichor) pen += 15;             // NPC.cs:81916\n  if (flags.brokenArmor) pen += 20;       // :81919\n  if (flags.betsysCurse) pen += 40;       // :81922\n  pen += Math.trunc(defense * armorPenetrationPercent); // :81927 (int) 截断\n  if (pen <= 0 || defense <= 0) return 0; // :81929\n  if (pen > defense) return (defense / 2) | 0; // :81933 整除\n  return (pen / 2) | 0;                   // :81935 整除\n}"
}
```

```
178:export class Arrow extends Entity {
316:  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
326:    const pd0 = projectileData(projId);
795:        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
=== 900-930 ===

  /** 回收补弹（ItemCheck_Shoot 1156 专支 Player.cs:47768-47786：生成
   *  3−在场数 条；速度分量各 +Next(−40,41)×0.05 扰动；出生点=玩家枪口。
   *  无重播 UseSound——原版 channel 期 itemTime 恒被 SetDummyItemTime(5) 顶住，
   *  无第二次 use 起手声） */
  private pTopUp(game: GameHooks): void {
    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number } | null;
    const ents = (game as unknown as {
      entities?: { projectiles?: unknown[]; add?: (e: unknown, b: string) => void };
    }).entities;
    if (!p || !ents?.add) return;
    const alive = (ents.projectiles ?? []).filter(
      (o) => (o as { projId?: number }).projId === PIRANHA_PROJ && !(o as { dead?: boolean }).dead).length;
    const aim = this.piranha!.aim();
    const a = Math.atan2(aim.y - p.cy, aim.x - p.cx);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3 - alive; i++) {
      const np = new Arrow(p.cx + Math.cos(a) * 14, p.cy - 4 + Math.sin(a) * 14,
        Math.cos(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
        Math.sin(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
        this.damage, this.knockback, PIRANHA_PROJ, null,
        { grav: 0, life: PIRANHA_LIFE, piranha: this.piranha! });
      np.critChance = this.critChance;
      np.critBonus = this.critBonus;
      np.armorPen = this.armorPen;
      np.frostEligible = this.frostEligible;
      np.pShootSpeed = this.pShootSpeed;
      ents.add(np, 'projectiles');
    }
  }

  private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {
=== 1050-1080 ===
    // 小动物接触（Damage_PVE 对 NPC 通用：小动物 friendly=false 亦受击；
    //  penetrate=−1 弹体不消耗；非 CanBeChasedBy 目标不会成为咬住对象）
    hitCritters(this, game);
    // 命中结算（Damage_PVE :11850 迭代 + Damage_PVE_Inner :11888）
    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
      const e = ent as unknown as PiranhaTarget;
      if (e.dead) continue;
      if (!(this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x
        && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y)) continue;
      if (this.pHitCd.has(e.id)) continue;                   // :11869 localNPCImmunity 门
      if ((e.iframes ?? 0) > 0) continue;                    // :11905 npc.immune[owner] 门
      // StrikeNPC :12811（暴击链同通用弹：基 4% + spawn 注入系暴击）
      const crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);
      const dmg = crit ? this.damage * 2 : this.damage;
      // StatusNPC :10622-10625：命中必挂 Hemorrhage(375) 240-360t（表驱动）
      applyProjStatus(PIRANHA_PROJ, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
      if (this.frostEligible) {
        applyFrostBurn((game.player as unknown as { equipStats?: { frostBurn?: boolean } })
          .equipStats?.frostBurn ?? false, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
      }
      playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
      // pierce=1 档：命中不设敌方免疫帧（:13154-13158 immune[owner]=0 语义），
      // fromPlayer=true 走 DPS/掉落门链
      e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(e.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback, 0, game, this.armorPen, crit, 1, true);
      game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.cy, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
      this.pHitCd.set(e.id, PIRANHA_HIT_CD);                 // :13157 localNPCHitCooldown=14
      if (this.pAi1 === 0) { this.pAi1 = 1; this.pTarget = e; }   // :12447-12450 首咬 latch
    }
  }
}
```

```
  private contact(game: GameHooks, dmg: number, cd: number, pad = 0): boolean {
    const add = (e: unknown, b: string) =>
      (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, y: string) => void } }).entities.add(e, b);
    let anyHit = false;
    for (const e of game.enemies()) {
      const en = e as never as EnemyLike & import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged & {
        hurtFx?: (d: number) => void;
      };
      if (en.dead) continue;
      if (!(this.x - pad < en.x + en.w && this.x + this.w + pad > en.x
        && this.y - pad < en.y + en.h && this.y + this.h + pad > en.y)) continue;
      if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, cd)) continue;
      // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价）

      const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en, dmg, game,
        (el) => add(el, 'projectiles'), SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, en.cx, en.cy);
      // 击退 = 武器 kb×0.5（本作接触系数）+ minionKB 加算（GetWeaponKnockback :52477-52482：

      const owner0 = this.ownerRef();
      const mkb = (owner0 as unknown as { equipStats?: { minionKB?: number } } | null)?.equipStats?.minionKB ?? 0;
      const pen0 = (owner0 as unknown as { equipStats?: { armorPen?: number } } | null)?.equipStats?.armorPen ?? 0;
      en.hurt(res.dmg, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * (this.knockback + mkb), 0, game, pen0); // 裸kb+minionKB(:52477)——0.5 自造已摘
      applyProjStatus(this.projId, en as unknown as Record<string, number>); // StatusNPC(:10555)
      this.attackFlash = 24;
      anyHit = true;
    }
    return anyHit;
  }

  /** 同类计数（计数器/本体伤害档位用：831 宝石数 / 970 阿比盖尔数） */
  private peerCount(game: GameHooks, id: number): number {
    let n = 0;
    for (const e of (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: unknown[] } }).entities.projectiles) {
      const m = e as MinionProj;
      if (m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === id) n++;
    }
    return n;
  }
```

```
export type { WhipTagged } from './WhipTag';

/** 2D 向量最小形状 */
interface V2 { x: number; y: number }
const v2 = (x: number, y: number): V2 => ({ x, y });
const lerpV = (a: V2, b: V2, t: number): V2 => v2(a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t);
const rotV = (ang: number): V2 => v2(Math.cos(ang), Math.sin(ang));
/** Vector2.RotatedBy(angle, origin) */
const rotAround = (p: V2, origin: V2, ang: number): V2 => {
  const c = Math.cos(ang), s = Math.sin(ang);
  const dx = p.x - origin.x, dy = p.y - origin.y;
  return v2(origin.x + dx * c - dy * s, origin.y + dx * s + dy * c);
};
/** Utils.Remap（value∈[from1..to1] → 线性映射到 from2..to2，区间外截断） */
const remap = (value: number, from1: number, to1: number, from2: number, to2: number): number => {
  if (value <= from1) return from2;
  if (value >= to1) return to2;
  return from2 + (to2 - from2) * (value - from1) / (to1 - from1);
};

export class WhipProj extends Entity {
  w = 18; h = 18; // DefaultToWhip :10096-10097
  dead = false;
  /** 原版鞭投射物 id */
  projId: number;
  /** 鞭物品 id（tag 查表） */
  itemId: number;
  damage: number;
  knockback: number;
  /** 词缀 bonusTagDamage（I:567——Game 侧注入，命中并进 whipTagDmg） */
  bonusTagDmg = 0;
```

```
/** 素材自洽 lint(tools/atlas-lint.mjs 的 vite 钩子;照搬 l10nAuditAuto 双消费:
 *  dev 警告 / build 阻断;vitest 不走钩子,由 tests/atlas-lint.test.ts 锁定)。 */
function atlasLintAuto(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'atlas-lint-auto',
    async configureServer() {
      const { auditAtlas } = await import('./tools/atlas-lint.mjs');
      const r = auditAtlas();
      for (const e of r.errors) console.warn(`[atlas-lint] ERROR ${e.rule} sheet=${e.sheet ?? '-'} ${e.msg}`);
      for (const w of r.warns) console.warn(`[atlas-lint] warn ${w.rule} ${w.key ?? ''} sheet=${w.sheet ?? '-'} ${w.msg}`);
      if (r.errors.length) console.warn(`[atlas-lint] ${r.errors.length} error —— 构建将阻断`);
    },
    async buildStart() {
      const { auditAtlas } = await import('./tools/atlas-lint.mjs');
      const r = auditAtlas();
      if (r.errors.length) {
        throw new Error(`[atlas-lint] ${r.errors.length} 处 error(素材自洽)——先修再构建:\n` +
          r.errors.map((e) => `  ${e.rule} sheet=${e.sheet ?? '-'} ${e.key ?? ''} ${e.msg}`).join('\n'));
      }
      console.log(`[atlas-lint] ${r.sheets} 表全自洽 ✓ (${r.warns.length} warn)`);
    },
  };
}
```

```
/** NPC.checkArmorPenetration 1:1 纯函数（NPC.cs:81913-81938）。
 *  原版语义：返回值是【加到伤害上的加成】（StrikeNPC :12808 num12 += 本值，
 *  之后 CalculateDamageNPCsTake 才减 def/2——Main.cs:65626-65635），等价于
 *  有效防 = def − min(pool, def)。pool 单池累加（无"每层独立"档）：
 *    armorPenetration（StrikeNPC :12068 = 玩家 GetArmorPenetration(melee) +
 *    弹幕自带 armorPenetration——数据层 vanillaProjectiles.armorPenetration）
 *    + ichor 15（:81916）+ brokenArmor 20（:81919）+ betsysCurse 40（:81922）
 *    + (int)(defense × armorPenetrationPercent)（:81927，闪电光环 688/690 传 1）。
 *  早退：pool ≤ 0 或 defense ≤ 0 → 0（负防/零防不吃穿甲加成）；
 *  超防钳制：pool > defense → defense/2（加成永不把伤害推过全额）。
 *  ★待接线：Enemy.hurt 现为"armorPen 减防 + ichor 平推 +7 直伤"近似（池外混算），
 *  2026-08-14 Enemy.ts 热文件禁改——冷却后替换 hurt 内 :6551-6557 段为
 *  dmg + npcCheckArmorPenetration(def, armorPen, penPercent, flags) 结算链 */
export function npcCheckArmorPenetration(
  defense: number,
  armorPenetration: number,
  armorPenetrationPercent = 0,
  flags: { ichor?: boolean; brokenArmor?: boolean; betsysCurse?: boolean } = {},
): number {
  let pen = armorPenetration | 0;
  if (flags.ichor) pen += 15;             // NPC.cs:81916
  if (flags.brokenArmor) pen += 20;       // :81919
  if (flags.betsysCurse) pen += 40;       // :81922
  pen += Math.trunc(defense * armorPenetrationPercent); // :81927 (int) 截断
  if (pen <= 0 || defense <= 0) return 0; // :81929
  if (pen > defense) return (defense / 2) | 0; // :81933 整除
  return (pen / 2) | 0;                   // :81935 整除
}
```

```
## 四项考古结论 + 改法 + 测试 + 隔离

### 项1 — Ichor 穿甲（projStatus.ts:30）

**考古**：原版 NPC 侧 Ichor **不是"防-15"也不是"平推+7直伤"**，而是 `NPC.checkArmorPenetration`（NPC.cs:81913-81938）单池加成：`pool = armorPenetration + ichor15(:81916) + brokenArmor20(:81919) + betsyCurse40(:81922) + (int)(def×pct)(:81927)`，早退 `pool≤0||def≤0→0`，超防钳制 `pool>def→def/2`，否则 `pool/2`（整除）——该值**加到伤害上**（Projectile.cs:12808 `num12 += …`），之后才 `dmg - def/2`（Main.cs:65626 CalculateDamageNPCsTake）。玩家侧 Ichor 才是 statDefense-15（Player.cs:9756-9758，Buffs.ts 已 1:1）。

**改法**：① projStatus.ts:30 注释按真实语义重写并登记 Enemy.hurt 现状偏差；② 新增 `npcCheckArmorPenetration()` 1:1 纯函数（整除/早退/钳制逐行行号）。**Ichor 施加点全查**：表驱动 278/279/280(600t)/479(420-840t)/524(180-420t) ✓、浸剂 meleeEnchant5 = 60×Next(10,20)t（Player.cs:6155-6158）= repo `10+rand*10` 等价 ✓、Game.ts:12061 挥砍 ✓、玩家侧 def-15 ✓——均无偏差。**唯一残差在 Enemy.hurt:6557（`ichorT?7:0` 池外平推）——Enemy.ts 为热文件禁碰**，已把 drop-in 补丁写进 npcCheckArmorPenetration 注释：`dmg = max(1, round(dmg + npcCheckArmorPenetration(def, armorPen, penPercent, {ichor,brokenArmor,betsy}) - def*0.5))`，冷却后 3 行接入。

### 项2 — armorPen 独立字段（Arrow.ts:769）

**考古**：弹幕自带 `armorPenetration`（Projectile.cs:260，SetDefaults 逐型直写）与玩家 `GetArmorPenetration(melee)`（Player.cs:4170-4177）在 StrikeNPC:12068 相加后同入 checkArmorPenetration 池——**独立来源、单池封顶，无"每层"档**。Arrow.ts:769 旧注释"独立 -10 防/层"描述本身错：Betsy's Curse(203) 是 flag 单例（NPC.cs:93287-93289）+池内 +40，711 弹 AddBuff(203,600)（:10719-10721），repo 现实现已 1:1 → **改注释销项**。

**改法**：① 提取器 NUM_FIELDS 加 `armorPenetration` 重生成 JSON——**恰 25 款入表**（85=15、916=50、917/1036=30、1045=50、1106=5…），其余零 diff；vanillaProjectiles.ts 补类型；② Arrow 新增 `settlePen = armorPen + 本型号 AP`，两个 hurt 结算点切换；③ MinionProj.contact pen0 改两源相加，并摘除 917/1036 的 `dmg0+30` 平推直伤近似（原版是穿 30 防非 +30 伤）。其余弹幕类（MagicProj/Dart/MissileProj/SwingArc 族）同配方一行接入，登记后续项；148 remixWorld 条件档=提取器既有限制，登记。

### 项3 — Buffs.ts:386,390

**考古**：玩家侧 Bleeding(30) **无直伤 DoT**——仅 `bleed → lifeRegenTime=0`（Player.cs:18998-19001，163 Obstructed 同语义 :11421-11425）；repo 的 lifeRegenTime 爬坡模型已在 Player.ts:1206-1230 落地 → ":386 sinceHurt 近似"注释已过时，**改注释销项**（Slimed(137) 翻倍仍 GAP）。vampireSeed 分支找到：Player.cs:18798-18813 `Main.vampireSeed → lifeRegen-=100`（50 HP/s，slime 再翻 -200），仅 buff 24、onFire3(323) 不吃。

### 项4 — WhipProj.ts:291

**考古**：`ApplyWhipDebuffs`（Projectile.cs:11067-11090）全集=913→323/240、912→324/240、914→1/5×20/180、1033→362/240、849→310/240。**"午夜鞭5320"是误记**：5320=PlaceableHealingPotion；849=ScytheWhip(Dark Harvest)。310/362 在 NPC 侧**非 DoT**：310=纯视觉粒子（:92148）、362=链电标记（:93298→ApplyEelWhipDoT :92992，每 60t 放电至多 3 只 600px 内标记怪，依赖 TagEffectState 5478——整块登记 GAP）。
```
